0x002b3200 faq.a.ghost_mode
0x002b3220 Ghost actions force specific lamps OFF 
0x002b324a  they override the car so a light that's normally on goes dark.
0x002b328b For this to hold, keep that action running in Loop (the repeat icon). The ghost action re-sends the "off" command on every loop, so the lamp stays dark for as long as the loop runs. Stop the loop and the car takes the light back.

0x002b41ce So such actions (especially headlight ones) only work while the car is stationary.

0x002b4440 faq.a.animations_not_working
0x002b4460 The same car could be built with different headlight types (e.g., halogen or Full LED), even though the main module (FEM/BDC) is identical.
0x002b44ed For example, the HIGH_BEAM_LEFT command directly controls the bulb on a halogen car and will work.
0x002b4551 But on Full LED, a separate module (FLE/FLM) handles the headlights, so the same command does nothing.
